Provenance note: Body text unaltered including its own OCR errors
  (Latin). Ribadeneira's original Latin life of Ignatius,
  commissioned by the 3rd Superior General - an early,
  primary-adjacent biography by someone who knew Ignatius
  personally. No PD English translation was located this pass.
  Vendored 2026-09-25 as part of a post-Step0-Revision-2 PD
  source-acquisition pass. Per this project's own INTAKE.md rule,
  this Latin text is a second witness, never primary evidence for
  a Representative.
